का . मा , 2978 : - - पतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन 
( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का भजन ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 
का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के 
ऊर्जा मंत्रालय , पेट्रोलियम विभाग की अधिसूचना का . प्रा . सं . 1589 
तारीख 25 मई, 1995 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से 
मंलग्न अनुसूची में विनिविष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग के अधिकार को 
पाइपलाइनों को बिछाने के लिए अर्जित करने का प्रपना माशय घोषित 
कर दिया था ; 


And whercas the Computent Authority has 
under sub -coction (1) (of Section 6 of the said Act , 
submitied report to the Government ; 


And further wierens the Contral Government 
has, oftor considering the said report droided 
to acquire the right of user in tho lands specified in 
the schedule appended to this notification ; 


और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त प्रधिनियम की धारा 6 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट देदी है । 


और प्रागे , यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने 
के पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुपूत्री में विनिर्दिष्ट भमियों 
में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का विनिश्चय किया हैं : 


Now , the efore , in exercice of the power 
conferred by sub - faction ( 1) of the Section 6 of 
the said Act, the Central Government hereby declares 
that the right of user in the said lands sporificd in the 
schedule appended to this notification hereby acquired 
for laying the pipeline ; 


अब, प्रतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा 
प्रवास शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा घोषित 
करती है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त 
ममियों में उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के 
लिए एमद्वारा अनि न किया जाता है । 


और मागे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रवत शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हार केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त भूमियों का 
उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने की बजाय तेल 
और प्राकृतिक गैस कार्पोरेशन में सभी बाधाओं से मुक्त रूप में , 
घोषणा के प्रकाशन की इस तारीग्य को निहित होगा ।

shall instead of vesting in the Central Government

और नक्त पाइप लाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के 
लिए अधि पना के संलग्न अनुसूची में वणित भमि में 
उपयोग के अधिार का अर्जन करना आवश्यक है ।

अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हए उनमें उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन करने के 
अपने श्राशय की घोषणा करती है । 

उका अनुसूची में वणित भूमि में हितबद्ध कोई 
व्यक्ति उस तारीख से जिसको , राजपत्र में यथा प्रकाणित 
इस अधिसूचना की प्रतियां साधारण जनता को उपलब्ध 
करा दी जाती हैं , 21 दिन के भीतर भूमि के नीचे 
गास नाइन बिछाने के संबंध में उन में उपयोग के अधि 
अधिकार का अर्जन करने संबंधी लिखित रूप में प्राक्षेप

( बिहार ) को कर सकेगा ।

$ .O . No. 2983 : — whereas it appears to the Central Govren 
ment that it is necessary in the public interest that for the 
transport of Petroleum and Natural Gas from Gandbar 
NTPC Tap off to G . T .E . C . Ltd , in Gujarat Statc pipeline 
should ne laid by the Gas Authority of India I.td . 

And whcreas it appears that for the purpose of laying such 
pipeline, it is necessary to acquire the right of user in the 
land described in the schedule annexed hereto ; 

Now , therefore , in cxcrcise of the powers conferred by sub 
section (1 ) of the Section 3 of the Petroleum and Minerals 
Pipelines (Acquisition of Right of User in the Land ) Act , 
1962 (50 of 1962), the Central Government hereby declares 
its intention to acquire the right of user therein ; 

Provided that any person interest in the said land may 
within 21 days from the date of this notification, object to 
the laying of the pipeline under the land to the Competent 
Authority , Gas Authority of India Ltd ., Darpan Building 
R . C . Dutt Rd. Vadodara . 

And every person making such an objection shall also 
state specifically whether he wishes to be heard in person or

का . प्रा . 2985. - केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता 
है कि लोकहित में ऐसा करना आवश्यक है कि पश्चिमी बंगाल 
राज्य के हल्दिया से बिहार राज्य के बरौनी तक पेट्रोलियम 
( क्रूड ) के परिवहन के लिए इंडियन प्रायल कारपोरेशन लि . 
द्वारा पाइपलाइन बिछाई जाए । 

और यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐमी पाइपलाइन बिछाने 
के प्रयोजन के लिए इस अधिसूचना के उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में 
वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का पर्जन करना आवश्यक 


अस , अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार, पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइप 
लाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम 
1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 )

अधिकार का अर्जन करने के अपने आशय की घोषणा करती 


उक्त अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में हितबद कोई व्यक्ति , 
उस तारीख से , जिसको भारत के राजपत्र में यथा प्रकाशित 
इस अधिसूचना की प्रतियां साधारण जनता को उपलब्ध

का , मा . 2986: - केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता 
है कि लोक हित में ऐसा करना प्रावश्यक है कि पश्चिमी 
बंगाल राज्य के हल्दिया से बिहार राज्य के बरौनी तक पेट्रो 
लियम ( ऋड ) के परिवहन के लिए इंडियन ऑयल कारपो 
रेशन लि . द्वारा पाइपलाईन बिछाई जाए ; 


[ सं . पार- 31015/ 11/ 96- ओ . प्रार .-- - 1 ] 

के . सी , कटोच , अवर सचिव 


और उक्त पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए 
अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग के 
अधिकार का प्रर्जन करना आवश्यक है ; 


New Delhi, the 14th October, 1996 


S . 0 . -- 2985 - Whercas, it appears to the Central Govern 
ment that it is necessary in the public interest that for the 
transport of petroleum ( crude ) - from- Haldia in the state of 
West Bengal to Barauni in the state of Bihar , pipeline should 
be laid by the Indian Oil Corporation Limited ; 


प्रतः, अब, केन्द्रीय सरकार , पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाईप 
लाईन ( भमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन ) अधिनियम , 
1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) 
धारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए उनमें उपयोग के 
अधिकार का प्रर्जन करने के अपने प्राशय की घोषणा करती 


Agd, whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying 
such pipeline it is necessary to acquire the right of user in 
the land described in the Schedule annexed to this notification ; 


Now , therefore, in extrcise of the powers conferred 
by sub -section ( 1 ) of section 3 of the Petroleum and Minerals 
Pipeines ( Acquisition of the right of User in Land ) Act, 
1962 ( 50 of 1962 ), the Central Government hereby declares 
its intention to acquire tho right of user tharcin ; 


Any person interested in land described in the said schedule 
may within 21 days from the date on which the copies of this 
notification , as published in the Gazette of India , are made 
available to general public , objóction in writing to the ac 
quisition of the right of user thereid or laying of the pipe 
line under the land to Shri Biswanath Bose, Competent 
Authority, Indian Oil Corporation Limited , Haldia - Barauni 
Crudo Pipolinc Project, P .O . Haldia Rofinbry , Distt. Midna 
pur , West Bengal. 


उम्त अनु सुम्मी में वर्णित भुमि में हितबय कोई व्यक्ति 
उस तारीख से जिसको, राजपत्र में या प्रकाशित इस अधि 
सूचना की प्रतियां साधारण जनता को उपलब्ध करा दी जाती 
है , 21 दिन के भीतर भूमि के नीचे पाइप लाइन बिछाने 
के संबंध में उनमें उपयोग के अधिकार का - मकरने संबंधी 
लिखित रूप में प्राक्षेप. श्री विश्वनाथ बोस, सक्षम प्राधिकारी 
इंडियन आयल कारपोरेशन लिमिटेड, हल्दिया; बधनी 
पाईपलाईन परियोजना, डाक घर हल्दिया रिफाइमरी ,जिला

ment that it is necessary in the public interest that for the 
transport of petroleum (crude) from Haldia in the State of 
West Bengal to Barauni in the State of Bihar, pipeline should 
be laid by the Indian Oil Corporation Limited ;

sub -section (1 ) of section 3 of the Petroleum and Minerals

(50 of 1962 ), the Central Government hereby declares its

का . आ . 2988.--- केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत 
होता है कि लोकहित में ऐसा आवश्यक है कि कि पश्चिमी 
बंगाल राज्य के हल्दिया से विहार राज्य के बरौनी लक 
पैट्रोलियम ( ऋड ) के परिवहन के लिए इंडियन प्रायल 
कारपोरेशन लि० रा पाइपलाइन बिछाई जाये ; 


और यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी पाइपलाइन बिछाने 
के प्रयोजन के लिए इस अधिसूचना में उपाबद्ध अनुसुची में 
णित भमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन करना आवश्यक 


है ; 


अतः, अब, केन्द्रीय सरकार , पैट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइप 
लाइन ( भमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम , 
1962, ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) 
धारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए , उसमें उपयोग

उस तारीख सें . जिसको भारत के राजपत्र में यथा प्रकाशित 
इस अधिसूचना की प्रतियां साधारण जनता को उपलब्ध 
फरा दी जाती है, इक्सीस दिन के भीतर , भूमि के नीचे 
पाइपलाइन बिछाने के संबंध में उसमें उपयोग के अधिकार 
का अर्जन करने संबंधी लिखित रूप में आक्षेप श्री विश्वनाथ 
बोस , सक्षम प्राधिकारी, इंडियन आयल कारपोरेशन लि०, 
हल्दिया बरौनी पाइपलाइन परियोजना, पोस्ट आफिस- हल्दिया

3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन जारी की गई भारत सरकार 
के पैट्रोलियम और प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना 
सं . का . प्रा . 588 तारीख 2 मार्च, 1096 द्वारा पश्चिमी

पैट्रोलियम ( ऋड ) के परिवहन के लिये पाइपलाईन 
बिछाने के प्रयोजन हेतु उस अधिसूचना के संलग्न अनुसूची 
में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार के अर्जन के अपने 
प्राशय की घोषणा की थी ।

12 मार्च, 1996 को उपलब्ध करा दी गई थी ।

अनुसरण में सक्षम अधिकारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी 
रिपोर्ट दे दी है ; 


और केन्द्रीय सरकार का उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने 
के पश्चात् यह समाधान हो गया है कि इरा अधिसचना 
में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिदिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग का

tial part of the sovereign functions of 
the State as a Welafre State . It is not, 
therefore , an industry . 


New Delhi by its Order No. L - 40011795 - IR 
(DU ) dated 27 / 30 -11- 1995 referred under Sec 
tions 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) and 2A of the Industrial Disputes 
Act, 1947 for adjudication of the industrial dis 
pute mentioned in the Schedule which reads as 
follows : 
“ Whether the action of the management of 

Telecom Distt. Manager Ananthapur is 
justified in terminating the service of Sri 
G . V . Subbaiah w .e .f. 1- 7 - 1986 ? If not, 
what relief he is entitled to ?" 


In view of the spccific findings of the Supreme 
Court, Telecom Department is not an industry . 
This Tribunal has no jurisdiction to adjudicato 
lipon the dispute between the Government Depart 
ment and its employee , 


This reference has been registered as Industrial 
Dispute No. 85 of 1995 on the file of this Tribu 
nal. After notice was issued by this Tribunal, to 
both the parties, the workman filed claim state 
ment and the Respondent filed a counter . 


2 . The learned Advocate for the Respondent 
Management requested this Tribunal to decide the 
legality of the reference as a preliminary point, in 
view of the fact that the Supreme Court held that 
the Telecom Department is not an industry within 
the meaning of Section 2 (j) of the Industrial Dis 
putes Act, 1947 . 


6 . However the learned Advocate for the Peti 
tioner workman raised several contentions. He 
aruged that the Supreme Court did not refer to the 
two leading decisions on the subject viz . Bangalore 
Water Supply & Sewerage Board v . A . Rajappa 
(AR 1978 S . C ., Page 969 - 1978 ( I) LLJ Page 
349 and also Chief Conservative of Forests v . 
Jagannath Maruti Kondhare and Others (1996 
S . C . C . ( L & S ) 500 ) which dealt with the sovereign 
functions of the State . He also referred to N . Nag 
endra Rao & Co. v. State of Andhra Pradesh ( 1994 
6 S . C ., Page 205 ) wherein the sovereign functions 
of the State are set out. He relied upon the deci . 
sion in Delhi Scicncc Forum v. Union of India 
and another ( AIR , 1996 S . C ., Page 1356 ) which 
upheld the new Telecom policy of the Government 
by which a part of tlie industry is entrusted to pri 
vate enterprises. He lastly argued at length and 
referred to several decisons referred to page 477 
of The A . I. R . Manual ( Vth Edition ) wherein the 
word "might" used in Section 11 of C . P . C . was 
considered . He argued that the Supreme Court 
might have or ought to have considered several 
other decisions on the subject and they were not 
considered . 


3. Both the parties are heard on the point. 


referred erprises. Hehe industry : 


4 . The point for consideration is whether this 
Trihunal has got jurisdiction to adjudicate the dis 


pute ? 


5 . POINT.-- - The Petitioner -workman was em 
ployed in the Telecom Department. The Govern 
ment of India made a reference to this Tribunal 
treating the Telecom Departnıent as an industry 
and the Petitioner as a workinan coming within the 
purview of Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 . The 
Supreme Court in Sub -Divisional Inspector of Post, 
Vaikam and Others v . Thevvan Joseph etc , 11996 
LL.R Page 483) , While deciding the case of Postal 
Department which is a part of Telecom Depart 
ment held as follows : 


7 . This Tribunal as a subordinate to High Court 
and Supreme Court cannot distinguish any deci 
sions given by Supreme Court directly on a parti 
cular point by referring to the decisions of the 
Supreme Court or of our High Court on a sinilar 
point, The Supreme Court held that the Telecom 
Department is not an industry . When once the em 
ployer is not an industry , there cannot be an in 
dustrial dispute and so this Tribunal cannot enter 
lain the dispute, if any, between tho Petitioner and 
the Respondent, ), therefore , hold that this Tribu 
nal has no jurisdiction to entertain this dispute. 


" Having regard to the contentions, the ques 

tion arices whether the andallant is an 
industry ? India as a sovereign socialist, 
secular democratic republic has to estab 
Jish an egalitarian social order under 
rule of law . The welfare measures part 
take the character of Soycreign func 
tions and the traditional duty to main 
tuin law and order is no longer the 
concept of State. Directive principles of 
State policy enjoin on the State diverse 
duties under Part IV of the Constitution 
and the performance of the duties are 
constitutional functions. One of the 
Cetyl is of the State to provide telecom 

nication service to the general pub 

lic and an emenity , and so is onc cssen 
2501 GI96 - - 16 


8 . In the result , an Award is passed according 
ly .

5 . The point for consideration is : Whether the action of 
the management of Central Hank of India , in disinissing 
from their services Sh . D . David w .e . f. 11th February, 1987 
is justifled ? If not, to what relief the workman concerned

6 . The Point : The workinan Shri David at the relevant 
linic was working as a Clerk in the Sivakasi branch of the 
respondent, Central Bank of India . When the workman was 
cmployed as a Clerk in the respondent-bank , he introduced 
ono Gurusamy who obtained a loan of Rs. 4 , 500 and the 
amount has to be repaid in 19 instalments. Mr. V . Guruşamy 
has alleged in his petition Ex . M . 2 , that he paid Rs. 200 
cuch in 8 instalments and when verified a sum of Rs. 700 
has not been paid by the workman towards the loan , The 
workman has admitted in the enguiry that Gurusamy was 
known to him for over 10 years, Due to the close association 
and that since the workinun way working in the said branch 
of the bank , Gurusamy handed over the amounts to the work 
man who defalcated to pay a sum of Rs. 700 into the ac 
count of V . Gurusamy. The main objection raised on the 
side of the petitioner - union was thut Gurusainy was not 
examined in the enquiry on the side of the management. How 
ever, the report given by Gurusamy and all other available 
been produced in the enquiry . Unlike a Criminal case , in 
& departinental enquiry any particular witness need not be 
examined provided the document would prove the charge. 
Accordingly when the documents have been marked and they 
revealed the preponderance of probability of the workman 
having recoived the amount from Gurusamy, and thereafter 
failed to deposit a sum of Rs. 700 the non -cxamination of 
Gurusamy was not immaterial. The workman has not shown 
any valid reason that the allegations made against him in 
Ex. M . 2 were falsc . On the contrary , he has sent a letter 
Ex. M .4 to the Branch Manager of tho respondent -bank seeing 
& weck s time to give his reply even thereafter he has not 
sent any reply . The Management sent Ex . M . 5 letter re 
minding him of the expiry of the period requested by him , 
and to submit his explanation forthwith . Thic workman sent 
a reply Ex. M .6 stating he had already sent an explanation 
on 9 - 9 -84. It was brought to the knowledge of the workman 
by the letter Ex. M .7 that 9 -9 - 84 was Sunday , and probably 
he could rot have sent reply on a holiday . The workman 
sent a reply Ex. M .8 saying that justead of 10 - 9 -84 he has 
written as 9 - 9 -84. The workman lias not inov d his little 
finger to show that it was a falsa complaint and on tho con 
trary he was gaining time by saying various reasons. His 
allegation that he had sent a reply on 9 - 9 - 84 has not heen 
proved and the circumstances show that he conld not havo 
sent any reply as alleged by him . Atove all, he has failed 
to produce a copy of the reply if at all he bed sent any such 
reply . So , it is clear that the workman had not shown any 
feasonable explanation for the allegation levelled against him 
in Ex. M .2 . MW1 Krishnamurthy the then brarch Manager 
of Sivakasi und MW2, Mahan have deposed in the departmen 
tal enquiry and documents have been marked through them , 
Their evidence is cogent and convincing and there is amnle 
materials y prove charge No . 1 against the workman . The 
non - examination of Gurusamy cannot be taken as a season 
to disbelieve the various documents and also the cvidence

S. O . 2998. --- Whereas the Central Government is satisfied 
that the publio interest requires that the services in the indus 
try engaged in the manufacture or production of minoral oil 
( Crude oil ) , motor and aviation spirit , diesel oil , Kerosene 
oil, fuel oil , diverse hydrocarbon oils and their blends in 
cluding synthetic fuels, lubricating oils and the like, which 
are covered by entry 26 in the First Schedule to the Indus 
trial Disputes Act , 1947 (14 of 1947 ) , should be declarel 
to be a public utility sorvice for the purposes of the said 
Act . 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub 
section ( vi) of clause ( n ) of Section 2 of the Industrial 
Disputes Act , 1947 (14 of 1947 ) , the Contral Government 
hereby declares with immediate effect the said Industry to 
bo a public utility service for the purpose of the said Act for 
a period of six months.

made a reference to this Tribunal by its Order No. L - 22012 :

dum dated 8th March, 1996 under Section 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) & 21 
of the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 for adjudication of the 
industrial dispute mentioned in its schedule which reads as 
follows : 
" Whether the action of the management in insisting 

wardboys of Aren Hospital, SCCL. RG. III, Gola 
varikbani, to shave the parts of the body as part of 
preparation of the patient for elective surgery is 
legal and justified ? If not, what relief the work 

men are entitled to ? " 
2 . After receipt of the above reference, this Tribunal issued 
notice to both the partios and tho Respondent has acknow 
ledged the receipt of the notice . The Advocato for the Poti 
tioner filed vakalat on 11th March , 1996 . For claim statement 
of the Petitionor the case was adjourned from time to time, 
No claim statement of the petitioner was filed . Hence it is 
found that the petitioner is not evincing any interest to 
prosecute his Alatter even though several adjournments were 
granted by this Tribunal to lead his cvidence and prosecute 
the matter . 

3 . On a perusal of the docket sheet, it is found that both 
parties are not evincing any interest to prasooute this matter . 
Hence there is no option except to close the reference . 
Hence the industrial dispute is closed ,

7 . At the outset it may be mentioned that the award of 
P . C . Sharma has not been filed . Hence it cannot be asccr 
tained whether this Tribunal had given any award under 
the facts and circumstances as they exist in the instant case . 
In the absence of award the contention of the oppos te party 
in this regard is repelled , 

8 . In the second place defence of settlement may be taken 
Lp . The settlements are alleged to have taken place on 
17 -11 - 87 , 27 - 10 -88 and in January , 1991 that is during the 
pendency of instant reference . In the case of Mis Hari 
Fertilizers Sahupuri versus State of U . P . 1991 Lah IC 777 
it has been held that when any settlement is arrived at 
during the existence of reference and the workman is not a 
party to it then such settlement will have no binding effect 
on tlic workman . In view of this authority I arrive at the 
conclusion that the above mentioned settlements will not 
be any impediment to the getting of any relief in the instant 
case .

" The truc test may be indlicated once again , 

where a worker or group of workers labours 
to produce goods or services and these 
goods or services are lor the business of all 
other, that other is, in fact, the employer. He 
has economic control over the workers sub 
sistence skill, and continued employment, 11 
he, for any reasons , chokes off , the worker is 
virtually laid -oll. The presence of intermedi 
ate contractors with whom alone the workers 
have inmediate or direct relationship ex 
contract is of no consequence when , on 
lifting the veil or looking at the conspectus 
of factors governing employment, we dis 
cover the naked truth , though draped in 
different paper arcanageinent, tha : the real 
employer is the management, not the imme 
Jiate contractor . Myriad devices , half-hid 
deu in fold after fold of legul form depending 
on the degree of concealmeut necdcu , 1ype 
of industry the local conditions and the 
like may be resorted to when labour legis 
latio . casts welfares obligations on the real 
cinployer, based on Arts . 38 , 39 , 42 , 43 
and 43A of the Constitution . The court 
inust be ustatu to avoid the mischief and 
achieve the purpose of the law and not be 

mislcd by the may of legal appearances " , 
and also my attention was drawn to the latest case of 
Gujarat Elecricity Board Thermal Power Station 
versus-Hind Mazdoor Sabha reported in 1995 Lab 
1. C . puge 2807 whcic the scope and nalure of Section 
10 of I. D . Act with reference to Contract Labour 
( Regulation and Abolition ) Act, 1970 have been con 
sidered and also in this case what will be the position 
of the workmen working under the contractor has 
also been discussed and decideci. 


22 . The question whether the permanent nature of 
Work requires that he should have worked throughout 
the year for getting the order of regularisation . But 
Their Lordships have decided that permavent work 
men did not require and such workmen should be 
employed throughout the year if the work in which 
he was angaged was of permanent nalure . 


23, It is needless to say that the job performed by 
thu is admittedly permanent and perennial nature and : 
thereby jrrespective of their continuance in job they 
should be considered to be the workmen according 
to the clelinition of the workman even under the 
I. D . Act and this gives the jurisdiction of this Tri 
bunal to decicto the question under Section 10 with 
reference to Section 25F of the I. D . Act. 


24 . Therefore , in sunuing lip the aforesaid dis 
cussions I have no liesitation iv hold that the con 
comed workmen of both references are the workmen

formed ihe job in perinanent and perenniul in nature

18 . Their Lordships ju the said case law have 
clcarly stated that the inain qucstion which is to be 
considered by the Tribunal whether the existence of 
the contractor is a camouflage and sham in order to 
defeat the right of the workmen . It is also decided 
that in that case the Tribunal has the power to decide 
the dispute under Section 10 . Their Lordships have 
also given guidelines with observation that in a pro 
hibited category of job no workinan can be appointed 
through contractor and that is against law and if the 
Tribunal is of the opinion that the existence of the 
contracto : or contract is sham or bogus or camou 
flagc to conccal the truth then the said contractor 
would be considered as merely intermediaries and the 
real employer would be the management for whom 
the workinen have worked . 


25 . As they were not under the employment of the 
management in regular manner but they have acquired 
right to be regularised in view of the law and trend 
of the decision of the Apex Court and I ain not in 
clined to give them any other relief except the relief 
of regularisation ini Cut, I in the manner as stated 
below . 


19 . In another case law reported in 1995 Lab . C . 
2064 (Parimal Chandra Raha & others --versus -Life 
Insurance Corporation of I. dia ), Hon ble Supremne 
Court has held that engagement of the Cantcen Wor 
kers through canteen committee or ohe employees of 
the cooperative society or contractors may not lead to 
the conclusion that the said committee or cooperative 
society are independent contractors, 


26 . Hence it is ordered that the demand of the

both the references as lenticned above on 
the roll of Balihari Colliery of M / s. B . C . C . L . 
is justified and the liablagenou is directed to regu

scale and benefits . In the meantime they are to 
employ as and when required on payment of wages 
at the rate of Cat . I. The managenicut is also directed 
to implementation of the award as pointed out 
withia 2 months from the date of publication of the 
Award . No cost is anded to any of the parties,
